###################################################
###################################################
###### Idea Variation 2 GA Idea Groups Admin
###################################################
###################################################

###############################################################
#### Select National Idea navy_1
###############################################################

iv2_ga_select_national_idea_navy_1 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly
	automation_tick_frequency = 1
	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_1 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_1_1 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_1_2 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_1_3 }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_navy
			var:iv2_var_progress_points_navy >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_not_reached_national_idea_limit
			var:iv2_var_current_national_idea_limit > var:iv2_var_national_idea_current
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_navy_1 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_1 
					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_1_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_navy_1
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_1
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_1
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					add = 1
				}	
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_progress_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### National Idea Level 2 navy_1_1
###############################################################

iv2_ga_select_national_idea_navy_1_1 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly
	automation_tick_frequency = 1
	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_innovation_points_for_nat_idea_navy
			has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_1
			var:iv2_var_innovation_points_navy >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_navy_1_1 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_1_1 
					years = -1 
				}
				remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_1
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_innovation_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_innovation_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
					add = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Deselect National Idea navy_1
###############################################################


iv2_ga_deselect_nat_idea_navy_1 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = never

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_1
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_1_1
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_1_2
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_1_3
			}		
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = iv2_tt_give_up_nat_idea_navy
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_1_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_1_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_1_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_1_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_1_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_1_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_1_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_1
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

#### Select National Idea navy_2

iv2_ga_select_national_idea_navy_2 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly

			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_2 }

				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_2_3 }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_navy

		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000

	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {

				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_2 
					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_2_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_navy_2
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_2
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_2
				}

				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly
	automation_tick_frequency = 1
	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_innovation_points_for_nat_idea_navy
			has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_3
			var:iv2_var_innovation_points_navy >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_navy_3_1 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_3_1 
					years = -1 
				}
				remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_3
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_innovation_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_innovation_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
					add = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Deselect National Idea navy_3
###############################################################


iv2_ga_deselect_nat_idea_navy_3 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = never

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_3
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_3_1
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_3_2
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_3_3
			}		
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = iv2_tt_give_up_nat_idea_navy
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_3_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_3_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_3_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_3_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_3_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_3_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_3_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_3
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Select National Idea navy_4
###############################################################

iv2_ga_select_national_idea_navy_4 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly
	automation_tick_frequency = 1
	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_4 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_4_1 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_4_2 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_4_3 }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_navy
			var:iv2_var_progress_points_navy >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_not_reached_national_idea_limit
			var:iv2_var_current_national_idea_limit > var:iv2_var_national_idea_current
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_navy_4 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_4 
					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_4_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_navy_4
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_4
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_4
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					add = 1
				}	
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_progress_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly
	automation_tick_frequency = 1
	player_automated_category = governmentreform

				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_5_2 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_5_3 }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_navy
			var:iv2_var_progress_points_navy >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy

			custom_tooltip = {

		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {

		}
	}

				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_5 
					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_5_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_navy_5
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_5
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_5
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					add = 1
				}	
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_progress_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

					name = iv2_var_innovation_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_innovation_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
					add = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### National Idea Level 4 navy_5_3
###############################################################

iv2_ga_select_national_idea_navy_5_3 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly
	automation_tick_frequency = 1
	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_innovation_points_for_nat_idea_navy
			has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_5_2
			var:iv2_var_innovation_points_navy >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_navy_5_3 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_5_3 
					years = -1 
				}
				remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_5_2
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_innovation_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_innovation_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
					add = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Deselect National Idea navy_5
###############################################################


iv2_ga_deselect_nat_idea_navy_5 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = never

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_5
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_5_1
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_5_2
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_5_3
			}		
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = iv2_tt_give_up_nat_idea_navy
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_5
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_5
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_5_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_5_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_5_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_5_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_5_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_5_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_5_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_5
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Select National Idea navy_6
###############################################################

iv2_ga_select_national_idea_navy_6 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly
	automation_tick_frequency = 1
	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_6 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_6_1 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_6_2 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_6_3 }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_navy
			var:iv2_var_progress_points_navy >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_not_reached_national_idea_limit
			var:iv2_var_current_national_idea_limit > var:iv2_var_national_idea_current
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_navy_6 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_6 
					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_6_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_navy_6
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_6
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_6
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					add = 1
				}	
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_progress_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = iv2_tt_give_up_nat_idea_navy
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_7
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_7
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_7_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_7_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_7_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_7_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_7_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_7_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_7_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_7
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Select National Idea navy_8
###############################################################

iv2_ga_select_national_idea_navy_8 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly
	automation_tick_frequency = 1
	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_8 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_8_1 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_8_2 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_8_3 }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_navy
			var:iv2_var_progress_points_navy >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_not_reached_national_idea_limit
			var:iv2_var_current_national_idea_limit > var:iv2_var_national_idea_current
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_navy_8 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_8 
					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_8_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_navy_8
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_8
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_8
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					add = 1
				}	
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_progress_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = never

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_8

				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_8_2
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_8_3
			}		
		}
	}
	
	effect = {

				if = {
					limit = {

						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_8_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_8_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy

					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_8_2

					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_8_2

						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy

					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy

	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_10 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_10_1 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_10_2 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_10_3 }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_navy
			var:iv2_var_progress_points_navy >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_not_reached_national_idea_limit
			var:iv2_var_current_national_idea_limit > var:iv2_var_national_idea_current
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_navy_10 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_10 
					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_10_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_navy_10
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_10
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_10
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					add = 1
				}	
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_progress_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### National Idea Level 2 navy_10_1
###############################################################

iv2_ga_select_national_idea_navy_10_1 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly
	automation_tick_frequency = 1
	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_innovation_points_for_nat_idea_navy
			has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_10
			var:iv2_var_innovation_points_navy >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_navy_10_1 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_10_1 
					years = -1 
				}
				remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_10
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_innovation_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_innovation_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
					add = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Deselect National Idea navy_10
###############################################################


iv2_ga_deselect_nat_idea_navy_10 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = never

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_10
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_10_1
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_10_2
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_10_3
			}		
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = iv2_tt_give_up_nat_idea_navy
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_10
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_10
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_10_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_10_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_10_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_10_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_10_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_10_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_10_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_10
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

			var:iv2_var_innovation_points_navy >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_navy_12_1 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_12_1 
					years = -1 
				}
				remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_12
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_innovation_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_innovation_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
					add = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Deselect National Idea navy_12
###############################################################


iv2_ga_deselect_nat_idea_navy_12 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = never

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_12
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_12_1
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_12_2
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_12_3
			}		
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = iv2_tt_give_up_nat_idea_navy
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_12
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_12
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_12_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_12_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_12_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_12_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_12_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_12_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_12_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_12
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Select National Idea navy_13
###############################################################

iv2_ga_select_national_idea_navy_13 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly
	automation_tick_frequency = 1
	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_13 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_13_1 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_13_2 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_13_3 }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_navy
			var:iv2_var_progress_points_navy >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_not_reached_national_idea_limit
			var:iv2_var_current_national_idea_limit > var:iv2_var_national_idea_current
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_navy_13 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_13 
					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_13_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_navy_13
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_13
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_13
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					add = 1
				}	
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_progress_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

					name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
					add = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {

			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_innovation_points_for_nat_idea_navy
			has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_14_2
			var:iv2_var_innovation_points_navy >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_navy_14_3 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_14_3 
					years = -1 
				}
				remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_14_2
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_innovation_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_innovation_points_navy
				change_variable = {

###############################################################
#### Deselect National Idea navy_14
###############################################################


iv2_ga_deselect_nat_idea_navy_14 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = never

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_14
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_14_1
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_14_2
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_14_3
			}		
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = iv2_tt_give_up_nat_idea_navy
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_14
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_14
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_14_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_14_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_14_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_14_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_14_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_14_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_14_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_14
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Select National Idea navy_15
###############################################################

iv2_ga_select_national_idea_navy_15 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly
	automation_tick_frequency = 1
	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_15 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_15_1 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_15_2 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_15_3 }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_navy
			var:iv2_var_progress_points_navy >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_not_reached_national_idea_limit
			var:iv2_var_current_national_idea_limit > var:iv2_var_national_idea_current
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_navy_15 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_15 
					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_15_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_navy_15
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_15
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_15
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					add = 1
				}	
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_progress_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_16
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_16
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_16_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_16_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_16_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_16_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_16_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_16_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_16_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_16
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Select National Idea navy_17
###############################################################

iv2_ga_select_national_idea_navy_17 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly
	automation_tick_frequency = 1
	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_17 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_17_1 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_17_2 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_17_3 }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_navy
			var:iv2_var_progress_points_navy >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_not_reached_national_idea_limit
			var:iv2_var_current_national_idea_limit > var:iv2_var_national_idea_current
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_navy_17 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_17 
					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_17_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_navy_17
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_17
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_17
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					add = 1
				}	
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_progress_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_innovation_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy

			}
		}

###############################################################
#### Deselect National Idea navy_17
###############################################################


iv2_ga_deselect_nat_idea_navy_17 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = never

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_17
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_17_1
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_17_2
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_17_3
			}		
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = iv2_tt_give_up_nat_idea_navy
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_17
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_17
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_17_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_17_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_17_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_17_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_17_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_17_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_17_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_17
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_19 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_19_1 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_19_2 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_19_3 }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_navy
			var:iv2_var_progress_points_navy >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_not_reached_national_idea_limit
			var:iv2_var_current_national_idea_limit > var:iv2_var_national_idea_current
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_navy_19 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_19 
					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_19_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_navy_19
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_19
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_19
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					add = 1
				}	
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_progress_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

iv2_ga_select_national_idea_navy_19_1 = {

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly
	automation_tick_frequency = 1
	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_innovation_points_for_nat_idea_navy
			has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_19
			var:iv2_var_innovation_points_navy >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_navy_19_1 = yes
		}

				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_19_1 
					years = -1 
				}
				remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_19
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_innovation_points_navy

					add = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}

###############################################################
#### Deselect National Idea navy_19
###############################################################


iv2_ga_deselect_nat_idea_navy_19 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = never

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_19
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_19_1
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_19_2
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_19_3
			}		
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = iv2_tt_give_up_nat_idea_navy
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_19
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_19
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_19_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_19_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_19_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_19_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_19_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_19_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_19_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_19
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

			var:iv2_var_innovation_points_navy >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_navy_21_1 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_21_1 
					years = -1 
				}
				remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_21
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_innovation_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_innovation_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
					add = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Deselect National Idea navy_21
###############################################################


iv2_ga_deselect_nat_idea_navy_21 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = never

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_21
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_21_1
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_21_2
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_21_3
			}		
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = iv2_tt_give_up_nat_idea_navy
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_21
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_21
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_21_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_21_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_21_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_21_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_21_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_21_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_21_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_21
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Select National Idea navy_22
###############################################################

iv2_ga_select_national_idea_navy_22 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly
	automation_tick_frequency = 1
	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_22 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_22_1 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_22_2 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_22_3 }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_navy
			var:iv2_var_progress_points_navy >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_not_reached_national_idea_limit
			var:iv2_var_current_national_idea_limit > var:iv2_var_national_idea_current
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_navy_22 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_22 
					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_22_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_navy_22
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_22
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_22
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					add = 1
				}	
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_progress_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

					name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
					add = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

			has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_23_2
			var:iv2_var_innovation_points_navy >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_navy_23_3 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {

		}

	effect = {

			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_23_3 
					years = -1 
				}
				remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_23_2
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_innovation_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy

###############################################################
#### Deselect National Idea navy_23
###############################################################


iv2_ga_deselect_nat_idea_navy_23 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = never

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_23
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_23_1
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_23_2
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_23_3
			}		
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = iv2_tt_give_up_nat_idea_navy
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_23
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_23
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_23_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_23_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_23_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_23_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_23_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_23_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_23_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_23
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Select National Idea navy_24
###############################################################

iv2_ga_select_national_idea_navy_24 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly
	automation_tick_frequency = 1
	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_24 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_24_1 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_24_2 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_24_3 }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_navy
			var:iv2_var_progress_points_navy >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_not_reached_national_idea_limit
			var:iv2_var_current_national_idea_limit > var:iv2_var_national_idea_current
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_navy_24 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_24 
					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_24_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_navy_24
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_24
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_24
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					add = 1
				}	
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_progress_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_25

					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_25
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_25_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_25_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_25_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_25_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_25_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_25_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_25_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_25
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Select National Idea navy_26
###############################################################

iv2_ga_select_national_idea_navy_26 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly
	automation_tick_frequency = 1
	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_26 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_26_1 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_26_2 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_26_3 }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_navy
			var:iv2_var_progress_points_navy >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_not_reached_national_idea_limit
			var:iv2_var_current_national_idea_limit > var:iv2_var_national_idea_current
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_navy_26 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_26 
					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_26_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_navy_26
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_26
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_26
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					add = 1
				}	
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_progress_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

				change_variable = {

###############################################################
#### Deselect National Idea navy_26
###############################################################


iv2_ga_deselect_nat_idea_navy_26 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = never

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_26
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_26_1
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_26_2
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_26_3
			}		
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = iv2_tt_give_up_nat_idea_navy
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_26
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_26
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_26_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_26_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_26_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_26_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_26_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_26_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_26_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_26
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_28 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_28_1 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_28_2 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_28_3 }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_navy
			var:iv2_var_progress_points_navy >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_not_reached_national_idea_limit
			var:iv2_var_current_national_idea_limit > var:iv2_var_national_idea_current
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_navy_28 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_28 
					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_28_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_navy_28
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_28
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_28
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					add = 1
				}	
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_progress_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Deselect National Idea navy_28
###############################################################


iv2_ga_deselect_nat_idea_navy_28 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = never

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_28
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_28_1
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_28_2
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_28_3
			}		
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = iv2_tt_give_up_nat_idea_navy
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_28
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_28
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_28_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_28_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_28_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_28_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_28_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_28_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_28_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_28
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

			var:iv2_var_innovation_points_navy >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_navy_30_1 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_30_1 
					years = -1 
				}
				remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_30
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_innovation_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_innovation_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
					add = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Deselect National Idea navy_30
###############################################################


iv2_ga_deselect_nat_idea_navy_30 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = never

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_30
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_30_1
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_30_2
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_30_3
			}		
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = iv2_tt_give_up_nat_idea_navy
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_30
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_30
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_30_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_30_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_30_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_30_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_30_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_30_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_30_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_30
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Select National Idea navy_31
###############################################################

iv2_ga_select_national_idea_navy_31 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly
	automation_tick_frequency = 1
	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_31 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_31_1 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_31_2 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_31_3 }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_navy
			var:iv2_var_progress_points_navy >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_not_reached_national_idea_limit
			var:iv2_var_current_national_idea_limit > var:iv2_var_national_idea_current
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_navy_31 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_31 
					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_31_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_navy_31
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_31
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_31
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					add = 1
				}	
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_progress_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

					name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
					add = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################

	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	allow = {
