			text = iv2_tt_no_innovation_points_for_nat_idea_navy
			has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_32_2
			var:iv2_var_innovation_points_navy >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_navy_32_3 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_32_3 
					years = -1 
				}
				remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_32_2
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_innovation_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {

				change_variable = {

###############################################################
#### Deselect National Idea navy_32
###############################################################


iv2_ga_deselect_nat_idea_navy_32 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = never

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_32
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_32_1
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_32_2
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_32_3
			}		
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = iv2_tt_give_up_nat_idea_navy
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_32
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_32
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_32_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_32_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_32_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_32_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_32_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_32_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_32_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_32
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Select National Idea navy_33
###############################################################

iv2_ga_select_national_idea_navy_33 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly
	automation_tick_frequency = 1
	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_33 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_33_1 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_33_2 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_33_3 }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_navy
			var:iv2_var_progress_points_navy >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_not_reached_national_idea_limit
			var:iv2_var_current_national_idea_limit > var:iv2_var_national_idea_current
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_navy_33 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_33 
					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_33_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_navy_33
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_33
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_33
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					add = 1
				}	
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_progress_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_34
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_34
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_34_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_34_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_34_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_34_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_34_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_34_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_34_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_34
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Select National Idea navy_35
###############################################################

iv2_ga_select_national_idea_navy_35 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly
	automation_tick_frequency = 1
	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_35 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_35_1 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_35_2 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_35_3 }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_navy
			var:iv2_var_progress_points_navy >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_not_reached_national_idea_limit
			var:iv2_var_current_national_idea_limit > var:iv2_var_national_idea_current
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_navy_35 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_35 
					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_35_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_navy_35
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_35
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_35
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					add = 1
				}	
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_progress_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_innovation_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy

			}
		}

###############################################################
#### Deselect National Idea navy_35
###############################################################


iv2_ga_deselect_nat_idea_navy_35 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = never

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_35
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_35_1
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_35_2
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_35_3
			}		
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = iv2_tt_give_up_nat_idea_navy
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_35
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_35
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_35_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_35_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_35_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_35_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_35_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_35_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_35_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_35
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_37 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_37_1 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_37_2 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_37_3 }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_navy
			var:iv2_var_progress_points_navy >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_not_reached_national_idea_limit
			var:iv2_var_current_national_idea_limit > var:iv2_var_national_idea_current
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_navy_37 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_37 
					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_37_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_navy_37
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_37
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_37
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					add = 1
				}	
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_progress_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

	ai_tick = monthly

			var:iv2_var_innovation_points_navy >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
			}

				}
				remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_37

###############################################################
#### Deselect National Idea navy_37
###############################################################


iv2_ga_deselect_nat_idea_navy_37 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = never

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_37
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_37_1
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_37_2
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_37_3
			}		
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = iv2_tt_give_up_nat_idea_navy
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_37
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_37
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_37_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_37_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_37_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_37_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_37_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_37_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_37_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_37
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

			var:iv2_var_innovation_points_navy >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_navy_39_1 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_39_1 
					years = -1 
				}
				remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_39
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_innovation_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_innovation_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
					add = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Deselect National Idea navy_39
###############################################################


iv2_ga_deselect_nat_idea_navy_39 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = never

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_39
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_39_1
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_39_2
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_39_3
			}		
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = iv2_tt_give_up_nat_idea_navy
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_39
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_39
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_39_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_39_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_39_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_39_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_39_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_39_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_39_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_39
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Select National Idea navy_40
###############################################################

iv2_ga_select_national_idea_navy_40 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly
	automation_tick_frequency = 1
	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_40 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_40_1 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_40_2 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_40_3 }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_navy
			var:iv2_var_progress_points_navy >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_not_reached_national_idea_limit
			var:iv2_var_current_national_idea_limit > var:iv2_var_national_idea_current
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_navy_40 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_40 
					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_40_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_navy_40
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_40
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_40
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					add = 1
				}	
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_progress_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

					name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
					add = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	allow = {

			text = iv2_tt_no_innovation_points_for_nat_idea_navy
			has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_41_2
			var:iv2_var_innovation_points_navy >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_navy_41_3 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_41_3 
					years = -1 
				}
				remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_41_2
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_innovation_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_innovation_points_navy
				change_variable = {

###############################################################
#### Deselect National Idea navy_41
###############################################################


iv2_ga_deselect_nat_idea_navy_41 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = never

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_41
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_41_1
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_41_2
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_41_3
			}		
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = iv2_tt_give_up_nat_idea_navy
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_41
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_41
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_41_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_41_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_41_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_41_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_41_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_41_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_41_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_41
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Select National Idea navy_42
###############################################################

iv2_ga_select_national_idea_navy_42 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly
	automation_tick_frequency = 1
	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_42 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_42_1 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_42_2 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_42_3 }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_navy
			var:iv2_var_progress_points_navy >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_not_reached_national_idea_limit
			var:iv2_var_current_national_idea_limit > var:iv2_var_national_idea_current
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_navy_42 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_42 
					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_42_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_navy_42
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_42
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_42
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					add = 1
				}	
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_progress_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_43
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_43
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_43_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_43_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_43_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_43_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_43_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_43_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_43_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_43
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Select National Idea navy_44
###############################################################

iv2_ga_select_national_idea_navy_44 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly
	automation_tick_frequency = 1
	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_44 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_44_1 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_44_2 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_44_3 }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_navy
			var:iv2_var_progress_points_navy >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_not_reached_national_idea_limit
			var:iv2_var_current_national_idea_limit > var:iv2_var_national_idea_current
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_navy_44 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_44 
					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_44_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_navy_44
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_44
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_44
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					add = 1
				}	
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_progress_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Deselect National Idea navy_44
###############################################################


iv2_ga_deselect_nat_idea_navy_44 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = never

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_44
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_44_1
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_44_2
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_44_3
			}		
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = iv2_tt_give_up_nat_idea_navy
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_44
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_44
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_44_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_44_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_44_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_44_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_44_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_44_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_44_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_44
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_46 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_46_1 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_46_2 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_46_3 }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_navy
			var:iv2_var_progress_points_navy >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_not_reached_national_idea_limit
			var:iv2_var_current_national_idea_limit > var:iv2_var_national_idea_current
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_navy_46 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_46 
					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_46_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_navy_46
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_46
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_46
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					add = 1
				}	
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_progress_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Deselect National Idea navy_46
###############################################################


iv2_ga_deselect_nat_idea_navy_46 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = never

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_46
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_46_1
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_46_2
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_46_3
			}		
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = iv2_tt_give_up_nat_idea_navy
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_46
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_46
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_46_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_46_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_46_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_46_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_46_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_46_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_46_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_46
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

			var:iv2_var_innovation_points_navy >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_navy_48_1 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_48_1 
					years = -1 
				}
				remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_48
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_innovation_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_innovation_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
					add = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Deselect National Idea navy_48
###############################################################


iv2_ga_deselect_nat_idea_navy_48 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = never

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_48
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_48_1
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_48_2
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_48_3
			}		
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = iv2_tt_give_up_nat_idea_navy
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_48
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_48
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_48_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_48_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_48_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_48_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_48_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_48_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_48_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_48
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Select National Idea navy_49
###############################################################

iv2_ga_select_national_idea_navy_49 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly
	automation_tick_frequency = 1
	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_49 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_49_1 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_49_2 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_49_3 }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_navy
			var:iv2_var_progress_points_navy >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_not_reached_national_idea_limit
			var:iv2_var_current_national_idea_limit > var:iv2_var_national_idea_current
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_navy_49 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_49 
					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_49_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_navy_49
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_49
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_49
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					add = 1
				}	
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_progress_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

					name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
					add = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

			has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_50_2
			var:iv2_var_innovation_points_navy >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_navy_50_3 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {

			value = 1000
		}

	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_50_3 
					years = -1 
				}
				remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_50_2
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_innovation_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
				}

###############################################################
#### Deselect National Idea navy_50
###############################################################


iv2_ga_deselect_nat_idea_navy_50 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = never

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_50
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_50_1
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_50_2
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_50_3
			}		
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = iv2_tt_give_up_nat_idea_navy
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_50
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_50
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_50_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_50_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_50_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_50_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_50_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_50_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_50_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_50
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Select National Idea navy_51
###############################################################

iv2_ga_select_national_idea_navy_51 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly
	automation_tick_frequency = 1
	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_51 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_51_1 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_51_2 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_51_3 }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_navy
			var:iv2_var_progress_points_navy >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_not_reached_national_idea_limit
			var:iv2_var_current_national_idea_limit > var:iv2_var_national_idea_current
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_navy_51 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_51 
					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_51_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_navy_51
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_51
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_51
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					add = 1
				}	
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_progress_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_52
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_52
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_52_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_52_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_52_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_52_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_52_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_52_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_52_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_52
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Select National Idea navy_53
###############################################################

iv2_ga_select_national_idea_navy_53 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = monthly
	ai_tick_frequency = 36

	automation_tick = monthly
	automation_tick_frequency = 1
	player_automated_category = governmentreform

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_53 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_53_1 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_53_2 }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_53_3 }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_no_points_for_nat_idea_navy
			var:iv2_var_progress_points_navy >= var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_not_reached_national_idea_limit
			var:iv2_var_current_national_idea_limit > var:iv2_var_national_idea_current
			}
			iv2_sct_dnat_idea_navy_53 = yes
		}
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		add = {
			value = 1000
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			hidden_effect = {
				add_country_modifier = { 
					modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_53 
					years = -1 
				}
				set_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_53_1 
				set_variable = { 
					name = iv2_var_nat_navy_53
					value = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_53
				}
				add_to_variable_list = {
						name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
						target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_53
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					add = 1
				}	
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_sub_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_progress_points_navy
					subtract = var:iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
			text = iv2_tt_increase_points_navy
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
					add = iv2_progress_cost_increase
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################
#### Deselect National Idea navy_53
###############################################################


iv2_ga_deselect_nat_idea_navy_53 = {
	type = owncountry	

	ai_tick = never

	allow = {
		scope:actor = {
			OR = {
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_53
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_53_1
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_53_2
				has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_53_3
			}		
		}
	}
	
	effect = {
		scope:actor = {								
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = iv2_tt_give_up_nat_idea_navy
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_53
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_53
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_53_1
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_53_1
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_increase
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_53_2
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_53_2
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x2
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_53_3
					}
					remove_country_modifier = national_idea_modifier_navy_53_3
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_progress_cost_increase
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = iv2_var_national_idea_innovation_cost_navy
						subtract = iv2_innovation_cost_decrease_x3
					}
				}			
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_variable = iv2_nat_idea_select_navy_53_1 
				remove_list_variable = {
					name = iv2_picked_national_ideas_list
					target = flag:iv2_picked_nat_idea_navy_53
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iv2_var_national_idea_current
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
